And we all stood still, and my father said to the woman: "Behold, the soul of thy husband was glad when this man said that he was innocent concerning thee and did not draw near * and art thou able also to set his heart at rest and drink the holy oil and swear to him that thou art free from this suspicion? - if so, then | will undertake that he be reconciled with thee." And the woman spake to him angrily like Jezebel, who was not ashamed at Elias the prophet when he came to her at a certain time, but said to him: Thou art Elias and | am Jezebel; "| have said to this one whom God destroy before the sun set to-day, that | swear to thee in whatever place you


[p. 441] {#po.22.p441}
فقال لبا ابى اذا ما انا ارسلت واحضرت لك الزيت المقدس هل تقدرى انت تشربيه قالت نعم حقا يا ابى فارسل الى القس المبارك اليساوس قائلا له هات الزيت المقدس وتعال وعند ذلك حضر القس ومعه الزيت فتناوله ابينا بيده وقال للامرلا لعلّك ما تقدرى ان تشرييه لان هذا الزيت اهلك اناس كثير وانا اظن انك لست بريه من هذ التهمه ولا سالمه منها ولا تقدرى تحلفى فتقدمت تلك الامرلا اليه بجاره وقضت بيدها الوعا الذى كان فيه الزيت وارادت كسرها من لجاجتها في طلب الزيت وعند ذلك دعى ابى الرجل الذى ضاجعها وقال له تقدم الى هاهنا وقول لنا الحتق قدام هذا المجمع القول الذى قلته لنا داخل البيعه قدام المذبح ماذا فعات بهدد الزانيه الوقحه فتقدم ذلك الرجل ودافق الامرا بجميع ما فعل فيها من النجاسه والقبيح وزوحها قائم سمع وعند ذلك أمر بضرب الامراه وطردها من قدامه وان يذهبوا با الى الحاكم يجريسها ويشهرها وييتبر بها غيرها وطردها من بلادة الى حيث لا ترجع وقال لزوحا ان انت رمت ان تتزوج بامرآة اخرى فليس يحصل لك راحه فقال له ذلك الرجل اننى تبت بقوة الله ودبركة صلواتك
choose and | will drink the holy oil." And my father said to her: "When | send and present to thee the holy oil, art thou willing to drink it?" She said: "Yes verily, O father." And he sent to the blessed priest Elisha saying to him: "Bring the holy oil and come." And therewith came the priest * and he had with him the oil. And our father took it from his hand and said to the woman: "Perhaps thou art not able to drink for this oil has caused many to perish, and | suspect that thou art not clear from this charge, nor unblameable, nor able to take the oath." And that woman advanced boldly and took in her hand the vessel wherein was the oil and came near to breaking it because of her insistence in taking the oil. Then my father turned to the man who had been with her and said to him: "Come hither and tell us the truth before this assembly as to the word which thou didst say to us when thou didst enter the church and go before the altar, - what hast thou done with this impudent adulteress?" - And the man drew near and disclosed all that he had done to the woman in impurity and foulness, and her husband stood by and heard. And thereupon * he commanded the woman to be beaten and driven from before him, and that they should take her to the governor who would disgrace her and make her infamous and cause her to be a warning to others. Thus he expelled her from the country so that she did not return. And he said to her husband: "Art thou desirous to marry another woman so that thou hast no peace?" The man said to him: "| am repentant by the power of God and by the blessing of thy prayers. | will not marry another


[p. 442] {#po.22.p442}
انتى لا اتزوج بعد بامراه اخرى الى يوم وفاتى ولانه تمسك ان لا يختلط بزيجه امراه كما قال الى .يوم وفاته بالحقيقه عظيم هو مجدك يا ابينا القديس الظيم فى الابا اننا بيسنتاوس الراعى الصالح الامين على الرعيه الذى يدبر قطيع الخراف الذى للمسيج انت اب الابتام وقاضى الارامل والمهتم بشأن الفقرا والمساكين اذ يعوليم ونحظيهم ما يحتاجون اليه ومن هو فى هذا الزمان يشهك فى حسن عبادتك تعنايتك وصلاح رعيتك وكثره مراحمك للمساكين ايها البتول الطاهر واضع الناموس الذى اختاره الله راعيا لشعبه الرب يرحمنا بصلوته وشفر لنا خطايانا امين.
woman to the day of my death." And he was steadfast and was not joined in marriage to a woman until the day of his death in fact. Great is thy glory, O Anba Pisentius, great holy father amongst the fathers, faithful shepherd who didst rule the flock of Christ. Thou wast a father to the orphans, judge to the widows and distressed, one who cared for the poor and wretched, who directed them * and received them when they had recourse to him. And who at this time is like thee in the excellence of thy service and thy care, in the purity of thy pastorate and abundance of thy pity to the poor, O thou chaste virgin and law-giver whom God appointed shepherd of his congregation? May the Lord have mercy on us by his prayers, and forgive us our sins. Amen.
الاعجوبه السابعه والثلاثون لابينا القديس العظيم اننا بيسْتاون شفاعته تكون معنا الى الابد “امين.
The thirty seventh Wonder of our father the great saint Anba Pisentius, may his intercession be with us for ever. Amen.
قال القديس تاودوروس كان ذات يوم وانا ماضى معه من حبل شامه الى! دير القديس اننا موساس لكيماة اأخذ وصيه كالعاد» وارجع لماآ اقمت ذلك اليوم سائر4 فى الطريق ونحن” قد رجعنا مصعدين اشتدة عليناة الحر فى7 ذلك7 اليوم جدا ونا امشى8 صحته
Saint Theodore said: One day it happened that | was going with him from mount Shama to the monastery of the holy Anba Moses to take advice according to our custom, and | proceeded * that day and reached there travelling upon the road, and as we returned the heat was very severe


[p. 443] {#po.22.p443}
فاحترقت بالعطش حتى كادت روحى! تفاء قنى اما هو فحزن4 جدا وتوجع قله بحسرة عظيمه وتنهد بمرارهة لاحلىة ثم انفرد على" الى ناحيه اخرى البعد منى قليل ووقف يصلى الى الله7 بتضرع عظيم وابتهال كثير” وهو كتيب القلب قائلا الليم لا تعطى لقلبى هذا الحزن العظيم على كبرى ولا تدعنى اقيم بقيه ايام حياتى متالم القلب شديد الحسرا على هذا النفس الذى" يلك بسببى لانك"! يا رب تعلم لامتى توجت ونينما كانت يدانا مسوطه!! استجاب له3! الذى ستجيب لعبيد الحافظين وصايا ا ب العاملين بمرضاته القدوس المبارك الى الابد امين' فتطلع! عن .يمينه فرآى قلة ماء على راس الجل طيبه جدا وعليها حشيش"! كثير وعند ذلك اتى الى ومسك بيدى واقامنى6! وذهب بى الى تلك"! القلت الما فشرت منها وروت وسجدت ا الله كثير"! الذى انا مستعد لاسمه صائع العجائب والايات فى قديسيه ولما فقت واهتدى قلبى مكث على ذلك الما
upon us that day and | was walking with him and | was consumed with thirst until my spirit nearly left me. And he grieved very much and his heart was pained with distress and sorrow, and he sighed deeply for my sake. Then he went away from me a little distance in another direction and stood and prayed to God with great humility and earnestness, saying: "O God, do not give my heart this great sorrow beyond its power, do not let me remain the rest of the days of my life thus afflicted with great sorrow about that soul who are perishing on my account. Thou knowest, Lord, that for my people | go, yet whilst his (my?) hands are outspread do thou grant him what is asked for the service of those who obey the commandments of the Lord and do his holy and blessed precepts for ever. Amen." And he turned on his right hand and saw a vessel of water at the head of the mount * very clear and about it much grass. And then he came to me and took my hand and raised me and led me to the vessel of water, and | drank of it and was satisfied, and greatly praised God whose name | served, who doeth wonderous signs by his saints, and | recovered my heart and was restored by that water until sunset. And we


[p. 444] {#po.22.p444}
الى حين غروب الشمس رجعنا الى مغارتنا بسلام صدقونى ابها الاخوه المحبين للاله انه قرأ ارعة اناجيل عشر دفوع وعند ما خرحنا من مسكننا الى حين رجوعنا اليه بركانه
returned to our cave in peace. Believe me, O brethren, lovers of God, that he read the four gospels ten times, and then we left our dwelling until we returned to it again. May his blessing be with us and enfold us all. Amen.

### The thirty eighth Wonder of the great saint Anba Pisentius. May the
تكون معنا وتشملنا جميعنا أمين. الاعجوبه الثامنه والثلاثون للقديس العظيم انا بيسنتاون الدب يرحمنا ١ بصلواته وبخلصنا من العدو امين.
Lord have mercy on us by his prayers and preserve us from the enemy. Amen.
قال لى ايضا ابى القديس ان الى دفوع كثيره يتفق لى ان ادخل الى الاوديه واصوم الاسبوع كله متواليا الى يوم السبت من قبل ان اخرج واتفق لى ايض مرارا كثيره ان الجيل! يمتلى على وحوش كنين خبيثه وسباع ضاربه وبخوفونى ونثبوا علتى ليشقوا فؤادى ولما علمت انهم خيالات شيطانيه كنت اببط يداى4 واصلى الى الله وللوقت يرسل ملاكه الى3 فيبددهم جميعهم من9 قدامى صدقونى با اولادى ان ضعين كانا7 ملاامين خدمته؟ واذا هما يسعيا الى مغارته مرارالا كثيرن ولما علم ان فى10 الامر!! ضارا واختشى ان يضيع! منه اح تعبه فطلب الى الله تعالى! الا تعود الوحوش! اليه دقعه
My holy father said to me: * "Many times it happened that | went out to the wadi and fasted a whole week up to the sabbath before | ceased. And it often happened that that mountain was full of many fierce wild beasts and hungry lions who terrified me and threatened to tear my entrails, but | knew that they were diabolical phantoms. And | used to lift up my hands and pray to God, and at once he sent his angel and scattered them all before me." Believe me, my dear children, that two hyaenas used to do him service and often resorted to his cave, but when he knew that there was harm in the matter and feared that he might lose the reward of his work, he prayed to God most high not to send those wild beasts to him


[p. 445] {#po.22.p445}
الخرى؟ ولم يعودوا اليهلا فيما بعد سلواته تحرسنا من الشرير الى ألنفس الاخير. أمين. الاعجونه التاسعه والثلاثون لابينا القديس العظيم انا بينتان الرب .يرحمنا بصلواته امين.
again, and they did not return to him afterwards. May his prayers protect us from the crafty one to our last breath. Amen. * The thirty ninth Wonder of our father the great saint Anba Pisentius. May the Lord have mercy on us by his prayers. Amen.
اتفق لى ذات يوم وهو فى البريه الداخله يصلى وتعبد الى الله تعالى كعادته من كل قلبه والتفت وراى تنيناة عظيماةً مخوف جدا" لم يرى9 منذ قط شىء يشبيه ولا" مثله ولما0! نظر هاله امرى وخاف !! منه! وعند ذلك انتصب قائما فى الصلاه والابتهال الى الله تعالى والتضرع الكثير! من خوف ذلك التنين وللوقت 9! استجاب آ! له الرب ذلك الذى استجاب لدانيال النبى8! ولما عدت راجعا"! اليه لكيما0؟ افتقد! احواله من بعد ثلاثه ايام فوجدت ذلك التنين مطروح ميت فمضيت لكيمالة اشاهده صدقونى يا اخودة ان طوله يكون مقدارة سته اذرع وشبر ووحبه يشبه وجه القط 7" وله 8قشر
It happened to me one day that he was in the inner desert praying and serving God most high with all heart according to his custom, and | looked round and saw a very dreadful great serpent, never was seen one resembling it or like it, and when | saw it | was frightened, whilst he continued standing in prayer and making petition to God most high and supplication on account of the fear of the serpent. And at once the Lord answered him, he who had answered the prophet Daniel, and when | returned to him to enquire about his welfare three days later | found the serpent cast aside dead, and | went forth in order to see it. Believe me, brethren, its length was six cubits and a span, and its face was like the face of a cat, and it


[p. 446] {#po.22.p446}
عظيم على جسدة ومنظرلا فزع مخوف جدا حتى الدبان اقول فى قلبى اليس لبشه هذا سوى ذلك التنين النى كان بابل فى الزمان المتقدم كمل على هذا القديسن بالحقيقه! المكتوب انك تطى3 الافعى4 والحيه الجرده وتدوس الاسد والتنينة بشفاعة هذا القديس يففر لنا الرب جميع خطابانا امين.
had thick skin on its body and its appearance was very dreadful even to the flies. And | said in my heart: Is there any other serpent like it save the one which was in Babylon in ancient times? And there was in truth fulfilled concerning this saint what is written: "Thou shalt tread upon the viper and the basilisk, and trample on the lion and serpent May the Lord forgive us all our sins by the intercession of this saint. Amen.
الاعجوبه الارحون لابينا القديس انا بيسنتاون الرب يرحمنا وخفر لنا خطيانا امين.
The fortieth Wonder of our holy father Anba Pisentius. May the Lord have mercy on us and pardon us our sins. Amen.
حدتى قائلا انا اقول لك يا ابنى انى بليت الخيز بالماء ستة مرارا من قبل ان أكله فقات له انا لاى سبب يا ابى قال لى انت تعلم اننا نصلى قبل ان ناكل حسب ما هو مفترض علينا فى القوانين وكذلك انا اصلى قبل ان أكل الخز فيخطفوا عقلى الى فوق السما وامات قائما اصلى الى اوان الصبح وهكذا يتفق لى الى كمال الاسبوع وصير اللخز بغير احد احدا كله بركانه معنا امين.
He conversed with me saying: "I tell thee, O my son, that | have moistened bread with water six times before eating it." And | said to him: "Why, O my father?" He said to me: "Thou knowest that we pray before eating as the percept is laid down for us in the canons, and therefore | pray before eating bread. And my mind has been snatched up above the heavens * as | have leaned standing to pray in the time of the morning, and so it has happened to me at the end of the week that the bread becomes dry as no one eats it." May his blessing be with us. Amen.


[p. 447] {#po.22.p447}
الاعجوبه الحاديه والارعون لابينا انا بيسنتاوس بصلواته يحرسنا الرب من الشرير الى النفس الاخير أمين.
The forty first Wonder of our father Anba Pisentius. May the Lord deliver us by his prayers from the crafty one to our latest breath. Amen.
كان ايضا ذات يوم سرق انسان كاس فضه من البيعه وكسرا فاوحى الله لابينا القديى بسب ذلك الكاس واكشف له امرد فارسل القديس ودعى السارق سرا بالكتمان وطلب منه قائلا ايها الانسان رد الكاس الذى اخذته لكيما نعيده الى مكانه واصلى عنك الى الله تعالى ليغفر لك خطيتك لانها خطيه عظيمه اما هو فانكر ذلك وقال انى لم اسرق طول عمرى البته فقضى عليه عند ذلك ابى القديس اننا بيسنتاوس قائلا له هكذا الرب الاله النى انت تجاسرت انت وسرقت انية بيته وكسرتها بلا رحمه ينتقم منك ويكر رقبتك وحطم سائر جسدك صدقونى يا اخوا ان فى اليوم الذى قضى عليه هذا انزل الله به ضريه شديده ومصيبه عظيمه ولم يزل فى ذلك العذاب الشديد حتى اتى بالكاس اليه وخر ساجدا تحت قدميه وق بذنه وخطاياه ثم انه حلمالله بصلوات هذا القديسن يغفر لنا الرب جميع خطايانا.
One day also a man stole a cup of silver from the church and took it away. But God revealed to our father the saint about that cup and made clear to him its secret. And the saint sent and called the thief secretly and asked him saying: "O thou man, restore the cup thou hast taken away that it may be put back, and | will pray to God for thee to * the most high that he may pardon thee thy sin, for it is a great scandal." Then he denied and said: "Never have | stolen in all my life." And thereupon my father the holy Anba Pisentius delivered sentence saying thus to him: "The Lord God against whom thou art bold and hast stolen property of his house and injured it, he will take vengeance on thee without mercy and break thy neck and destroy all thy body." Believe me, my brethren, that in the day when he thus gave judgement, God sent down on him severe punishment and great chastisement, and there was no cessation from those severe torments until he brought the cup to him and was prostrate at his feet and confessed his fault and his sin. Then he was set free by the prayers of this saint. May the Lord pardon us all our sins.


[p. 448] {#po.22.p448}
الاعجوبه الثانه والارحون لابينا انبا بيسنتاون شفاعته تكون معنا الى الابد امين.
* The forty second Wonder of our father Anba Pisentius. May his intercession be with us for ever. Amen.
تحدثت انا معه ذات يوم وقلت له يا ابتاد اعطى لنفسك راحه وارتاح قليل لانك فى تعب عظيم من كثر» تردد الثاس اليك كل ايوم من الصبح الى الما ولا يدعوك تتعى ساعه واحده وفى الليل ايضا وليس تعطى لنفسك راحه ابدا كما نرى من صفرة وجهك وضعف جسدك اتراك انت اكثر خطيه من كل من يسكن على الارض فاحابنى قائلا اعلم انت امر الاسقفيه درك عظيم لانه اذا دخل الى اليوم انسان ونبى الى خن انسان انه ننى او يقول عن امراه انها تركت عنها زوجا وزنت مع اخر او يشتكى لى امن كبنه او ام رهبان فرطوا فى سيرتهم او صنعوا فى مجد رتبتهم اليس الواجب على ان اسطو عليهم بالعدل واعمل فييم ما قد امرت به القوائين المقدسه لثلا يسالنى الله عنهم ويطالبنى بخطاباهم اذا ما انا غفلت عنهم الرب الاله يرحمنا بصلواته وطلباته الى النفس الاخير امين.
| was conversing with him one day and | said to him: "O father, give thy self rest and relax a little, because thou art in great exhaustion by the excessive crowding of men about thee every day from morning to evening, and they do not let thee lie down for a single hour, and in the night also there is no rest ever allowed thee, as we see by the pallor of thy face and the weakness of thy body. Dost thou think thyself a sinner more than any who dwell on the earth?" And he answered saying: "Know that the work of a bishop is a great responsibility, for a man enters one day and he is informed that a man has committed fornication, or it is reported concerning a woman that she has forsaken her husband and commits * adultery with another, or there is complaint about the clergy, or about the monks that they are irregular in their life, or act for the glory of their order. Is it not compulsory that | act strictly towards them in justice and do to them what the holy canons commana, - lest God ask me about them and tax me with sins because | have neglected them?" May the Lord God have mercy on us by his prayers and petitions to our last breath. Amen.


[p. 449] {#po.22.p449}
الاعجوبه الثالثه والارعون لابينا انا بيسنتاوس. حض اليه ذات .يوم ارخن عظيم وهو حزين القلب جدا وطلب اليه قائلا يا سيدى الاب لنت اعلم ماذا اصنع من اموالى وسعة ارزاقى لانى اعطى فى كل سنه خراج كثير ولم يرزقنى الله تعالى ولدا وان انت صليت عنى لكى تلد زوحتى ولدا انا اعطى صدقات كثير للمساكين واحسن الى الغريا واعمل الخير مع جميع الناس الى .يوم وفاتى فدخل القديس الى خزانته وصلى على ماء قليل ودفعه له ثم اعطاد ثمرتين وقال له اعطيهم لزوحتك تاكل وتشرب هذا الماء وانت ايضا لاتدع شىء ر دى فى بيتك بل اطردٍ الشك والرب الاله يعطيك شهوة قلبك وانا اقسم لكم يا اخوتى واحباى ان مثل فى ذلك الوقت ثانى سنه رزقه الله تعالى ولدين ذكور فحمليم فى حضنه واتى بم الى ابى القديس وقال له خذ هذا المال واصرفه فى الصدقه فاحابه ابينا القديس وهو مغضب لانه ما كان يقل رشولا على مواهب الله ونعمته وقال له خذ مالك انت وصدق به حسب ما نذرت لله تعالى على نفسك وقدام الرب والخير يكون لك فى منزلك ان انت اخرجت الشك
The forty third Wonder of our father Anba Pisentius. One day there was present with him a great noble and he was very sad of heart and prayed him saying: "O sir, father, | do not know what to do with my wealth and the abundance of my substance, for every year | give many alms. But God has not granted me a son. If thou wilt pray for me that my wife may bear a son * | will give many favours to the poor and benefits to strangers, and will do well to all men to the day of my death." And the saint entered into his cell and prayed over a little water and gave it to him, then gave him two dates, and said to him: "Give them to thy wife, let her eat, and let her drink this water, and thou also do not be neglectful in thy house but repel doubt, and the Lord God will give thee the desire of thy heart." And | swear to you, my brethren and beloved, that at that time next year God most high blessed him with two male children, and he bore them in his bosom and brought them to my father the saint and said to him: "Take this wealth and bestow it in alms." And our father the saint answered and was angry because he would not accept a fee for the gift of God and his grace, and he said to him: "Take this wealth of thine and give alms of it as thou hast vowed to God most high * in thy soul and in the Lord's presence: and it will be better for thee in thy dwelling if thou pay the tax on thy house and do not invent excuses


[p. 450] {#po.22.p450}
من بيتك ولا تجد عله ونتهاون بما قد اوصيتك فيشتد حزنك جدا عاجلا عند جميع من ينظرك بركته تشملنا حميعنا الى الابد امين.
and neglect what is enjoined thee, lest your grief grow greater before all who observe thee.” May his blessing embrace us all for ever. Amen
الاعجوبة الرابعه والارحون لابيئا انا بيسنتاوسن.
The forty fourth Wonder of our father Anba Pisentius.
اسمعوا ايضا هذه الاعاجيب الذى اظهرها هذا القديين اننا بيسنتاون قبل وفاته واعطوا المجد للرب ابنا يموع المسيح كانت امرالا قد ولدت ونشف اللبن من ثدييها واسودوا واورموا جدا حتى صار كل ثدى مثل قالب الطوب الكبير وهما مفلقين فى عنتها اما ابوها وزوحها فانهم ايسوا منها واستقربوا موتها وحضروا الى عند ابينا القديس وتاركا من يديه وقالا له اصنع يا ابانا معنا محبه ورحمه وصلى على يسير من زت واعطيه لنا لكيما تدهن به هذه الصبيه وان ثدييها ورموا وهى قد قارب وانظر اليها بعين الرافه لان هوذا لنا ايام كثير؛ ونحن نطوف بولدها على النسوان المرضعات ليسقون اللبن اثلا يموت قارسل للوقت القس الى داخل المذبح واخذ قطعة خرتها ولها من زت القنديل وسراج المذبح واتى با اليه فمد أصبعه ورسمها قائلا باسم الاب والابن والروح القدس الاله الواحد هب
Hear also this wonder which this holy father Anba Pisentius displayed before his death, and give praise to the Lord our God Jesus Christ. There was a woman who bare a child and the milk dried in her breasts and they became black and hard greatly until both her breasts were like great bosses of brick, and their tops were chapped. Then her father and her husband despaired of her, they expected * her death, and brought her before our holy father and sought blessing at his hands and said to him: "O father, do an act of kindness and charity for us and pray over a little oil and give it to us that we may anoint this young woman with it, as her breasts have swollen and also she is near death; look on her with the eye of pity. Now we have been with her many days and we take her child round to women who give suck that they may give it milk lest it die." And at once he sent the priest to the entrance of the altar and he took a portion of rag and pierced it and moistened it with the oil of the lamp and the wick at the altar and brought it to her and touched her and made the sign of the cross on her saying: "In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. May he bestow remedy for all


[p. 451] {#po.22.p451}
الهفا لجميع المرضا الذين يتدهنون بهذا الزيت بامانه صحيحه واتوا بالقطعه الخرقه " المبلوله من الزيت المقدس تلك الثى باركها ابيا وصحوا با تدبى تلك الامراه المريضه وللوقت بيت ثدبها من المرض والورم ودرت اللبن للوقت حتى انصب من ديا الى اسفل الارشض ورضع الطفل منيا بخن فساد ولا مانع ولاعائق وكل من راها تعجب وسح الله ومجده بشفاعه هذا القديس ينعم لنا الرب بغفران خطايانا وبحربنا من الشيطان اللعين الى ابد الابدين امين. الاعجوبه الخامسه والارحون للقديس انا بيسنتاوس شفاعته معنا امين. قال القديس تادرس كان ذات يوم وانا جالس صحة ابى القديس العظيم انا بيسنتاوس فى حل شامه وكان هناك عابد عظيم قديس كبير يدعى اسمه ابيفانيوس متعبد الله بلا فتور. من. كل قله وقد شاع خبرلا عند كل احد فاتفق له زارته يوما لكيما يفتقد احواله وننال ببركته لان هذه كاات عادتنا ان نجتمع بعضنا بعض وكان وصانا الى مسنكه بدا ابى يناديه وهو خارج الاب كقانون البيعه والاخوه وقول له بارك على ثم دخانا
the disease of those who are anointed with this oil in perfect faith and repentance." And they brought the pierced rag * moistened with the holy oil which our father had blessed and anointed her, and that sick woman at once had her breasts healed from disease, and at once the teats gave forth milk so that it flowed down to the ground from her breasts, and the child sucked from her without injury and was not hindered or checked. And all who saw her wondered and praised God and magnified him. May the Lord by the intercession of this saint give us grace by the pardon of our sins and guard us from the accursed devil for ever and ever. Amen. The forty fifth Wonder of the holy Anba Pisentius. May his intercession be with us. Amen. The holy Theodore said: One day, | was sitting in the company of my father the holy and great Anba Pisentius in mount Shama and there was there a great devotee, * a leading saint whose name was Epiphanius, who served God without ceasing with all his heart and was celebrated everywhere. And it happened to him one day that he was on a visit to observe his condition and that we might enjoy blessing from him. And it befell when we reached his abode that my father began to call to him, whilst yet outside the door, according to the canons of the church and the brotherhood, "Bless me."


[p. 452] {#po.22.p452}
وكان القديس انا بينتاون يتلو فى سفر ارميا النبى العظيم وانا ابيفانيوس كان هو ايضا يتلو فى رسائل بولص السليح فتقدم ابى الى باب المغار الذى كان ابيفانبوس قائم من داخلبا يتلو فتطلع ابى الى داخل تلك المغاره وراى رجلين جالسين على المساطب وسكث هو ايضا يتلو من خارج الباب لان القانون الموضوع ليس .يحكم بدخوليم بعضيم بعض الى عند انقضا التلاوا فلما أكمل القديس ابيفانيوس تلاوة رسائل التليح بوالص دخل اليه ابى القديسن انا بيسنتاوس وسلما بعضهم على بعض وذلك الرحل الجالى قبل رؤسهما كلاهما ثم صلوا وجلسوا وغاب عنهم ذلك الرجل الذى كان حالس فدخات انا اليهم وتاركت منهما وهم جلوس الواحد مع الاخر يتحدثا فى عظائم الله ومواهبه الجزيله التى انعم بها على هؤلاء الاصفيا العظما لاحل الالام التى قيلوها على اسمه القدون ولما قالا عظائم الله طال الوقت عليهما وهما يتحدثان من الكتب المقدسه التى هى انفاس الله قاما وسلما بعضهما على بعض وانصرف ابيفانوس الى موضع اخر ولما دخنا الى الخزانه مكث قلبى متفكرا فسجدت عند قدمى ابينا انبا بينتاوس وقات فى إلى الحق انى ما ابرح ساجدا عند قدميك حتى تكمل لى مرادى اما هو فاعترف لى قائلا انى لست
Then we entered and the holy father Anba Pisentius was reciting from the book of Jeremiah, the great prophet; and Anba Epiphanius also was reciting from the epistles of Paul the apostle, and my father came forward to the door of the cell within which Epiphanius was standing and reciting, and my father looked into the inner part of that cave and he saw two men sitting upon the stone bench, and then he * paused reciting at the entry of the door, as the canon observed does not permit them to approach one another until they have finished reciting. And when the holy Epiphanius had finished reciting the epistles of the Apostle Paul, my father the holy Anba Pisentius went in to him and they saluted one another and that man who was sitting before them both: then they prayed and sat down and that man who was sitting there went away from them. And | entered to them and asked a blessing from them. And they were sitting by one another talking about the great things of God and the joyful gifts which he bestows upon his chosen friends because of the sufferings which they endure for his holy name. And when they had spoken about the great things of God for a long time they talked about the holy scriptures which God has inspired, then they rose up and saluted one another * and Epiphanius departed to another place. And when we entered into his cell my heart was reflecting anxiously and | threw myself at the feet of our father Anba Pisentius and said in much anxiey: "I will not cease to lie prostrate at thy feet until thou fulfil for me my request." And he informed me saying: "I will not


[p. 453] {#po.22.p453}
احزنك فيما سالتنى عنه فقات له يا ابى القديس من هو هذا الرجل الاحلح العغليم المجد الذى هذا مقداره الذى لك فى فمك وانت وابيفانيوس واختفى عنا فيما بعد قال لى انت استحقيت ان تنظرة فقلت له نعم يا ابينا حقا لقد نلت نعمه عظيمه ونات بركه حزيله لان هذا هو العظيم فى الرسل بولص كان جالس عند ابيفانيوس الى ان أكمل جميع تلاو رسائله وقبله وانصرف الى العلو اولا لاتعلم انت ابنى هذه هى عاده " القديسين أذا ما كان انسان طاهر نقى بقلبه وافكاره او يتحدث فى اخار القديسين فان كل واحد من القديسين يحض وبجلس عند وكل من يتلو فى اقولهم الى ان يكملوا لهم تلاقتهم فيقل ذلك القديس فمه ونصرف من عند وان كان الذى يتلو مستحق النظر للمشاهده اليه شاهده ذلك القديس بعينه مواحبه وكذلك الفصول التى تقراً فى البيع يكونوا واقفين إسمعونهم وسبحوا وسجدود صلواته تكون معنا امين. الاعجونه السادسه والارعون لاا بيسنتاون.
conceal from thee what thou dost ask me about." And | said to him: "My holy father, who is this so very bald man who has been this while with thee and did kiss thee on the mouth, both thee and Epiphanius, and did disappear from us?" And after a while he said to me: "Didst thou deserve to see him?" And | said to him: "Yes indeed, my father." (He said): "Verily thou hast obtained great favour and hast received signal blessing, for this was the great one among the apostles, Paul; he was sitting beside Epiphanius until he finished reciting all his epistle, then he kissed him and ascended up on high. First do thou know, O my son, * that this is the custom of the saints, and when a righteous man is reverent in his heart and mind and takes part in intercourse with the saints, then each one of the saints is present with him whilst he recites their words, that saint is present with him until he has finished his recitation and then he kisses his mouth and departs from him. And if he who recites is a man deserving to see the saint, that saint will look at him with his eye face to face. And so with the chapters which are read in church, they stand by and hear them and give praise and glorify." May his prayers be with us. Amen. The forty sixth Wonder of Anba Pisentius.
كان ذات يوم ونحن فى حيبل الاساس حضروا اليه جماعه ليتباركوا منه وبخروه بحزن
One day as we were in mount al-Asas there came a multitude to get his blessing, and they talked with him about the pains of their hearts and


[p. 454] {#po.22.p454}
قلوهم ولما بارتهم رآهم مخنلين فى عقوليم جدا قعلم بالروح انهم يريدوا يسالوا عن كلام فاراد ان يقوم ونصرف اماهم فمسكوه وطلبوا اليه وكان فى ذلك الوقت عند رجل « اهب وكان هناك ايضا القس موساس ركس دير انا صمويل المعروف بدير السند! فقالوا له يا ابانا القديس انا بيسنتاوس نحن حجنا اليك لكيما تروضنا وترسم لنا مرسوم ان لاتجزع عنه حسب ما يلهمك بروح الله قال لهم من اى اللاد انتم قالوا له هذين الائنين من ناحية اسوان والاثنين الاخر من اهل سقط. رشين ونحن شركا بعضنا مع بعض وعمانا واحد واشترينا كلنا بضاعه وحملناها فى المركب وسافرنا الى كور بعيده وام يحتمل احد منا شىء زائدا عن رفيقه واقمنا فى سفرنا سنه وثمانية اشهر ففتح الله علينا واستفدنا فائده عظيمه متضعفه مترادفه ورجعنا الى بيوتنا بسلام وحمانا الفين دينار وصرناهم فى كيس وسلمناهم لرجل مخصص من دونا اذ هو الكبير فينا فاخذهم وانكر وقال لنا انتم اخذتم صر« من الكيس فيها خمسماية دنار فاحجاب الشيخ المبارك القديس انا بيسنتاوس وقال هذا هو الحق الصحيح لانى انا قلت فى قلبى لو كان غريب الذى كان سرق فكان
when he had blessed them he perceived that they were distressed in their minds greatly, * and he knew by the spirit that they wanted to ask him about a matter, but he made as though to rise and depart. And they took hold of him and entreated him. Now at that time there was a monk with him, and there was also the priest Musas, head of the monastery of Anba Samuel which was known as the monastery of as-Sunad, and they said to him: "O holy father Anba Pisentius, we have come to thee that thou mayest do us good and sign us with the sign, do not be annoyed thereby, as the Spirit of God inspires thee." He said to them: "From what district are you?" They said to him: "These two are from the neighbourhood of Aswan, and the two others are of the people of Safat Rashin. We are all in partnership with one another and work together, we all share in goods and carry them by ship and journey to a remote country, and no one of us enjoys any preference over his companion; * and we went on our journey a year and eight months, and God made an opening for us and prospered us with great profit in return of increase. And we are going back to our homes in peace and bear two thousand dinars and have tied them up ina purse and have entrusted them to a man selected from amongst us who is our eldest: and he took them and denies them to us and says, "Some time you took from the purse five hundred dinars." And the elder, the blessed holy Anba Pisentius, answered and said: "This is the truth. In my heart | said, if it were a stranger who was the thief then he would


[p. 455] {#po.22.p455}
ياخذ الكل وان ابى عز عليك! ذلك وضاقت فى وحه جدا ولبث .يجول فى المكان كله ومضى ابينا القديس انا بيسنتاوسن الى مكان اخر منفرد وحده ليصلى ثم خرج الهم وقال لهم لقد انفسدت احوال الناس جميعا اخذ بيد ذلك الرجل واتفرد به الى ثاحيه على حد وقال له ليس لك ربح فى الغدر باخوتك وتحل عليك لغة الله فاكثر عند ذلك البرج والتشوش وقال .يا ابى ما دام هذا المال تحت يدى ما اقدر افطل شىء من هذا فلما را القديس ان ذلك الرجل قد جعل فى قله انه لا يظبر شىء دعى ثلاثه من الملاحون وانفرد بهم الى ناحيه وقال لهم انطلقوا هوذا ثلاثة كارات ثياب مربوطه من النى معكم اسفل فى الخنقا الذى فى البيعه منهم حلبا واخرج منها مخده فتجد ذهب هذا الرجل الشييخ وثيابه وقد حلوا بامانه ووجدوا الذهب ببينه فى المكان الذى قال ليم عنه ابونا القديس انا بيسنتاوس ثم ترددوا اليه فى جيل شامه يومئذ واعلموه بذلك فاحتبس واقام اياما كثيره لا يظبر لاحد من اناس اذ كان لا يريد ان يظبر عنه شىء من هذه الاشيا لانه كان بغض مجد الناس الباطل وحب مجد الله وحدا بركاته وصلواته تشمل جمعنا واولاد المعموديه امين.
have taken all." And this has troubled my father. His face became sad and he continued to go to and fro in the place. And our holy father Anba Pisentius went to another place by himself alone to pray, and then he returned and said to them: "Indeed the bundles of the men have been plundered altogether." * Then he took that man by the hand and led him apart by himself alone and said to him: "There is no profit to thee in treachery to thy brethren, and the curse of God will fall upon thee." Then he increased his noise and shouting saying: "O father, whilst this money was in my hands | could not do anything of this." And when the saint saw that the man had determined in his heart not to disclose anything he turned to three of the crew and called them to his side and said to them: "Take the three rolled up bundles of clothes which are in the pots which are in the church and untie them and take from them a roll and you will find in it the gold of this head man and his clothes." And they untied it in faith and found the gold in the place of which our holy father the Anba Pisentius had told them. And one day * they went to him in mount Shama and informed him of it, but he secluded himself and stayed many days concealed from the people, as he did not desire to make any of these things known, because he hated the empty praise of men and loved the approval of God alone. May his blessings and prayers embrace us all and the children of baptism. Amen.


[p. 456] {#po.22.p456}
الاعجوبه السابعه والارعون لابينا انبا بيسنتاوس صلاته معنا أمين
The forty seventh Wonder of our father Anba Pisentius. May his prayer be with us. Amen.
تحدث معى ايضا ذات يوم انا الحقير يوحنا وقال لى صدقنى يا ولدى انى قيلت اتعاب كثيره فى ابتدا دخولى فى هذه الرتبه التى هى الاسقفيه لاننى وحدت اناس كثير يتزوج الانسان منهم ابنه اخته ومنهم من يتزوج باختين ومنهم كثير يتزوج الانسان امراد يزوج انه لابنتها ومنهم من يستحل مضاجعة الام وابئتها ووجدت اناس كثير حال لا يعرفون ان ثم اله ولا يامنوا يقيامه ولا يحاسب ولا يعاقب وهم كمثل الهائم وهم مع ذلك متفقين بعضهم مع بعض على الفساد وقلة المعرفه حتى الكبنه وكبار الشعب وهم لا يدعونى انسان بالجمله لعن كانوا يسمونى المختل المجنون الذى لا يعرف ماذا يقول وان امنعهم قريوهم الكبنه والكبنه ايضا امنعهم وتصرفوا وصرفوا الشعب الذى امنعهم وتاومونى جميع ايام تحذرى لهم وسمونى بنتيون وسنده الذى من اهل شمير المختل الذى لا يعرف ماذا يقول وسمونى باسامى ستة وقولوا فى اقوال غربه الى ان اخرجوا قلبى اما انا فلقى جميع اهتمامى الى الرب الذى عليه اتكالى واصلى واقول .يا رب تتكون ارادتك ثم اقول ايضا ان بيسنتاوس لا يعنيه امر هؤلاء الجبال ولا يبالى بشىء من كلامبم ابدا بل تكون
One day again he conversed with me, the unworthy John, and said to me: "Believe me, my son, | endured many troubles at the beginning of my entering into this office of the episcopate. For | found many who had married a sister's daughter, and some who had married two sisters, and some who considered lawful the intercourse with a mother and with her daughter, * and | found that many men were ignorant and did not know about God, and do not believe in the day of resurrection, and do not take heed, nor act carefully. They are like the beasts and agree with them in wickedness and ignorance: even the clergy and the chief of the people. And men did not call me a man at all, but called me the muddled lunatic who does not know what he says: and if | forbade them, the clergy bring them nearer (to me), and | bade the clergy also that they go away and check the people whom | forbade. They call me "Pisentiun" and "Pisenda" who is one of the interfering busy-bodies who does not know what he says. They call me six names and speak in strange speech so as to vex my heart. But | refer all my anxieties to the Lord in whom | rely and hope and * say: "O Lord, thy will be done." Then also | said that Pisentius is not injured by the conduct of these foolish ones, nor does he


[p. 457] {#po.22.p457}
ارادة المسيح وتكون نواميس الرب قائمه وقوانين بيعته مقبوله ودعهم يعملوا بى ما استطاعوا وانا مستعد الصبر والاحتمال على جميع ما يقولوه عنى حتى .يرد الرب قلويم اليه ومظص نفوسيم الرب يرحمنا بصلواته وخفر لنا خطابانا وسامحنا امين.
at all heed their speech. May the will of Christ and the law of the Lord prevail and the canons of his church be accepted, and let them do against me whatsoever they will: | am ready to be patient and to endure in all they say against me until the Lord turn their hearts to him and finish their lives." May the Lord have mercy on us by his prayers and forgive us our sins and be gracious to us. Amen.
الاعجوبه الثامنه والارحون لاا بيسنتاوس. قيل+ عن ابيا القديس انا بيسنتاوس انه تحدث مع الاخو ذات يوم قائلا هوذا نحن تصنع عادتنا وخدمتنا بقدر قوتنا ونصلى4 ونصوم افتراة ان الله راضى علينا الان؟ وقبل” منا هذا9 لكنى لا9 افتر من السؤال والتضرع الى عظم جود وتحننه حتى اعلم أن كان هو راشي علينا وتسيب !1 صلواتنا ومتقل قرابيثنا18 وعاداتا!1 قام من مسكنه وسار الى الجل ليل ووقف17 يصلى وسط الحر تبار1 وليلا
The forty eighth Wonder of Anba Pisentius. It is said of our father the holy Anba Pisentius that he conversed one day freely with the brethren saying: "We carry out our customs and services according to our power, and pray and fast strictly that God may have mercy on us and accept us, but | will not cease from prayers * and humble supplications to the greatness of his generosity until | know that he has mercy on us and our prayers are answered and our offerings accepted." Then he rose from his place and went a little way into the


[p. 458] {#po.22.p458}
ولم يزال على! ذلك الى ان تكامات له اربعة عشر يوم بلياليهال وهو قائم بلا فتور ويداد مبسوطتان ولم يضما اليه البته ولوا فارقت ثقتى جندى لنت4 افتر ولا ابح من هاهنا حتىة يظهر لى ايه فى هذا" المكانة وسط هذا الوادى واعلم8 منها ان الله يستجيب لنا وبرحمنا ولما" كان اليوم الرابع"! عشر وهو واقفا على؟ قدميه!! يصلى فى شبر ونه ا وينما كان قائم ,تضرع وجبيع اهتمامه ملقى الى الرب8! خطف7! عقله ساعه18 سر وسمع صوته يقول له هفكذ يا بيستاوس قد سمع الرب طلتك!2 واستجاب صلواتك وقبل ابتهالك وتضرعك وسوف يكون لك ما قد سالت من الرب وتمنيت وقد يظبر لك عين9 ما فى هذا المكان الذى انت قائم فيه ونكون آيه ظاهرا وعلامه ابديه9 دائمه لجميع اجيال العالم9 لكل30 من يتناول ماهالا
mountain and stood and prayed while the heat was severe by day and night, and he did not cease until fourteen days and nights were complete. He stood without ceasing and his hands were spread out, he did not draw them back at all (saying): "Even though my soul leave my body | will not break my fast nor move from here at all until he show me a sign in this place and in the midst of this valley, so that | know from it that God answers our prayers and has mercy on us." And when it was the fourteenth day, he still remained on his feet and prayed, and it was in the month Bauneh. And whilst he was standing making humble prayer and all his attention was directed towards * the Lord, his mind was wrapped up for an hour and he heard a voice saying to him: "O Pisentius, the Lord has heard thy petition and answered thy prayers, and a sign is given thee, and what thou hast asked from the Lord shall be granted, and thou art favoured. He will show thee a spring of water in this place where thou art standing and there shall be a clear and lasting sign always for all


[p. 459] {#po.22.p459}
بامانه وينما هو قائم! يصلى انشقت الارض! تتحت قدميه وفاض الماء الى انا انتهى الى ركبتيه وهو واقف فى الصلاه بلا فتور ولو قد" كمل على هذا القديسن ما قله" عايد” البي ان الرب قرب من جمع الفين يمعوله إقاب" مسقيو وهو يصنع مسرة اصفياة وخائفيه وستجيب لم!! ويخصهم من !! جميع شدائدهم! وضوائقم من ذا الذى يشهك الذى صعدت عطرة طيب صلواتك الى الله لقد انك تشه ايليا النى ارتفعت صلواته الى علو السموات وانت على الحقيقه عديل هابيل البار الصديق الذى قبل الرب منه ذبائحه وقرابينه اذ كان قدمها بقلب مستقيم من ذا الذى ستطيع ان يحصى عجائمك وكثره القوات الذى احراها الله على يديك تمجيدا لاسمه والاعاجيب التى صنعتها فى حياتك والتى كانت ايضا بعد وفاتك ولم يكن احد من البشر يحصى عددها ولا يكتب حملتبها لكلى انا الحقير يوحنا تجاسرت جدا وكتنت اليسير مما قد شاهدته
generations of the world, to all who take its water in faith." And as he stood at prayer the earth parted beneath his feet and the water burst forth until it reached his knees: but he continued in prayer without stopping So the saint was answered as David the prophet said: "The Lord is near all those who call upon him with upright heart, and he gives joy to the righteous and meek and answers them in prayer and delivers them from all * their troubles, and perplexities Who is like thee who didst send up the sweet perfume of thy prayers to God, thou mayest be counted as resembling Elias the Tishbite whose prayers rose up to the heavens? Thou verily art equal to the upright Abel from whom the Lord accepted sacrifices and oblations because they were with upright heart. Who is able to reckon thy miracles and the multitude of mighty deeds which God vouchsafed at thy hands and which brought glory? And as to the miracles which thou didst in thy life, as well as those which were after thy death, no-one could count their number or write down their sum. But I, the poor John, have greatly ventured and have written a few of those which my eyes


[p. 460] {#po.22.p460}
عيناى وسمعته اذناى من القوم التقاة الصادتقين! ' فى اقواليم وبالعظم الفضائل الجزيله العاليه التى صنعها ابى فى ايام رهبنته الفاخره من قبل ان يرتفع الى رتيه الاسقفيه انى ولو صرت كلى لسان فلا يمكتنى ان اصفها لكم بالتمام والكمال والجمله لما تكامات اله ثلاثين _سنه وهو راهنا دعلا الله إلى رتنه! الاسقفيه على مدينه قفط4 واعمليا فيما بعد ولما كرز اسقف اقام فى اسقفيته ثلاثه وثلاثين سنه اض اسقفا ومعلما ومرشدا وراعى صالج يرعى شعبه بتقوى الله وخشيته حافظا لقوائين ابائنا الرسل الاطبار وبحفظ القوانين واجرى الله على يديه ايات ظاهرلا وعجائب باهر وقوات كثير لايحصى عددها وكان يشاهد مناظر عجيه وطلع على اسرار غامضه اذ هو كان فى الاوديه كل حين احتاج دفعه الى الما فاقام اسبوعا كاملا وهو لم يجده وعند ذلك وقف فى الوادى وصلى واذا الما قد طلع فى الوادى وفاض حتى امتلاء منه ذلك الوادى فملوا منه جميع اوعيتهم بقدر ما يكفى الاخوا السكان فى ذلك الجبل صلواته وركاته تكون معنا امين.
have seen and my ears have heard from righteous God-fearing men * in their statements, and how great and lofty were the virtues which my father practised in the days of his glorious monasticism before he was raised to the rank of the episcopate. If | were all tongue | could not do it for you completely and perfectly. When he had completed thirty years as a monk God called him to the dignity of a bishop over the city of Qift and its environs, and after he became bishop he exercised the episcopate another thirty three years as bishop and guide and good shepherd. guarding his people, and God gave him power and his fear to observe the canons of our fathers the holy apostles, and he observed the canons and God did conspicuous signs and splendid miracles at his hands and so many mighty deeds that one could not reckon their number. And he gave evidence by displaying miracles * and disclosing hidden secrets, when he was at all times in the oases. And once he was in need of water and remained a whole week without finding it. So he stood in the oasis and prayed, and as he recited in the oasis it began to flow until that oasis was filled with it, and they filled the tanks and wells with enough for the brethren dwelling in that mountain. May his prayers and blessings be with us. Amen.


[p. 461] {#po.22.p461}
الاعجوبه التاسعه والارعون لانا بيسنتاوس بركاته تكون معنا امين كان بينما هنو مختفى فى البريه يصلى وهو على صخا عليه الى انه بدا النور شرق فسمع اصوات رفيعه حتى كاد الجل يرتتج كله من تلك الاصوات بترئيل وتسبيح وامتلاه الجبيل جميعه من البروق الاريه والرعود القوبه وعبقت رائحه اطياب فاخ خالصه فوقع على عند ذلك سات فتطلعت ونظرت الجو كله ملائكه من الصفوف الروحائة العلوية وهم احزابا احزابا يسبحوا ورتلوا لابسين حلل المجد والكرامه بشرف لا يوصف ونود بى ومجد سمائى منهم الانبيا والرسل ورؤسا الابا والشهدا والقديسين والسواح والنساك والمجاهدين وعساكر الرب السمائين وابن الله حاضر معيم بوالدته العذرا الطاهره بجماتهم ولما شاهدهم سقط على وحبه مذعورا من الخوف حتى كاد يسلم الروح وللث يتضرع الى الله تعالى بكل الاتهال وساله ان يظهر له كنه مقدار هذا الامنر نهتف اليه صوت من فوق يقول له بينتاوس بيسنتاوس ما تعلم ان هذا يوم عيد وتذكار الشبيد المجيد بقطر بن رومانوس وهو الذى سال الله فارسل هؤلاء الاصفيا والشهدا والابرارد والقدرسين
The forty ninth Wonder of Anba Pisentius. May his blessing be with us. Amen. As he was hiding himself in the desert he prayed, and was upon a steep rock until the day break came. And he heard loud voices until all the mountain was shaken by those voices with singing and praise, and all the mountain was filled with lightnings and mighty * thunderings and a pure and extremely sweet perfume was diffused. Then | stood still and turned my attention and perceived in all the air angels in lofty spiritual ranks and in companies who praised and sang, wearing the garments of glory and dignity with splendour indescribable, and there was light and heavenly glory. Amongst them were the fathers and apostles, and patriarchs, and saints, and hermits, and ascetes, and athletes, and soldiers of the Lord of heaven, and the Son of God was present amongst them with his mother the pure Virgin. And when he beheld this he fell prostrate on his face from fear, so that his spirit was near departing, and he continued to make humble prayer and petition to God Most High with all supplication that he would show him what this thing was. And a voice cried to him from above and said * to him: "Pisentius, Pisentius, dost thou not know that this is the festival which commemorates the noble martyr Victor son of Romanus? - he has asked God and he has sent these pure ones and


[p. 462] {#po.22.p462}
ليأنوا الى بيعته وسيدرا وجلك انت ايضا مستحق مشاهدتهم ونال من بركاتهم اما القديس انما بيسنتاوس فانه مجد الله وقديسيه وتعجب من عظائم الله تعالى ورافته ومحيته وحدث الاخود بهذد الاشيا العظيمه لما حضروا عندة وقال لهم عظيما هو مجد الشهدا الذين قبلوا الالام وسفكوا دماهم على اسم نا يموع المسيح له المجد والاكرام والسجود الان وكل اوان والى دهر الداهرين وابد الابدين امين الاعجوبه الخمسون للقديس العظيم انا بيسنتاوس.
martyrs and righteous and saints to come to his church and visit thee, and has made thee worthy to see them and enjoy their blessing, because thou art the holy Anba Pisentius." And he glorified God and his saints and marvelled at the greatness of God Most High and his compassion and love. And he told the brethren these great things when they were present with him, and said to them: "Great is the glory of the martyrs who have endured trials and poured out their blood for the name of Jesus Christ our Lord, to whom be glory and honour and worship now and always and for all the ages, for ever and ever. Amen." * The fiftieth Wonder of the great saint Anba Pisentius.
صدقونى يا اخوتى واحباى ان هذا القديس حفظ ثلاثين كتب من كتب اليعه ومسكث يتلى فيها الى حين وفاته وقام عشر سنين هارب من قدام البرير الذين هم الفرس وكان يقيم ثلث السنه اربعة اشهر مختفى ولا يعلم احد اين مكانه سوى القس موساس وانا ايلياس الذى من دير السند وابو يحنس تلميذه الذى من حل شامه ومرارا كثيرلة كان يظهر للقس انا اندراس رئيس دير الصليب المقدس وهذا الكلام الذى كتبه والنذى انا مزمع ان اكتبه ايضا انا المحارب عند الله تعالى وبالحقيقه انى لو كتبت جميع القوات
Believe me, my brethren and beloved, this saint learned by heart thirty of the books of the church and continued to recite them up to his death. He fled for ten years before the barbarians, those were the Persians, and he was for a third of the year, four months, concealed and no-one knew where was his place except the priest Musas and Anba Elias who was of the monastery of as-Sanad and Abu Yuhannas his disciple who was of mount Shama: and very many times he showed himself to the priest Anba Andreas the head of the monastery of the Holy Cross. And this is the discourse which he wrote, and which | endeavour to write also, | the soldier of God Most High. And indeed if | wrote all the mighty deeds and


[p. 463] {#po.22.p463}
والبراهين التى صنعها والمجائب التى اظبرها كان القول يطول وبكثر الكلام وكل الوصاف ومل السامع لكننى اكتفيت بوصف النزر اليسير واستغنيت به عن طول الشرح لان الانسان اذا ما هو تعبد لله بكل قلبه وكل شىء يساله يعطيه له الرب لان رثا سورع المسيح هو امن واليوم وابد الابد اله ارواح الابا والانيا والرسل والشهدا والقدسين صانع الايات والعجائب على يديهم وهو الاله الحتق له المجد الى ابد الابدين امين. الاعجوبه الحاديه والخسون للقديس انا بيسنتاون
exploits * which he performed, and the miracles he displayed, the discourse would become too long and the oration protracted, so the describer would be tired and the hearer would be wearied. But | am content with reporting this brief discourse and have abstained from length of illustration, that if a man worshipped God with all his heart the Lord would give him everything he asked, for the Lord Jesus Christ, who is yesterday, to-day, and for ever God of the spirits of the fathers and prophets and apostles and saints, doing signs and wonders by their hands, he is the God of truth. To him be glory for ever and ever. Amen. The fifty first Wonder of the holy Anba Pisentius.
حدئتى ابينا قائلا انى مرارا كثيرة اجى الى البير ومعى حرتى لكيما املاها ما ولم اجد الدلو هناك على البير فابسط يدى الى الله تعالى بالصلاه والتضرع والابتهال وعند ذلك يصعد الما من قع البير وفيض الى فوق فاملا منه حرتى واصلى ايضا لتعود كما كانت اولا ثم احمل جرتى وانطلق بسلام بصلاه هذا القديس العظيم ليغفر لنا خطايانا الى الابد امي
Our father conversed with me saying: "I have often gone to the well and had with me my jar to fill it with water, but | did not find the bucket there at the well. And | lifted up my hands to God * Most High in prayer and supplication and entreaty. And thereupon the water rose from the bottom of the well and came up to the top, and | filled my jar with it and prayed that it should be again as it had been before. Then | took up my jar and went away in peace." By the prayer of this great saint may he forgive us our sins for ever. Amen.


[p. 464] {#po.22.p464}

### The fifty second Wonder of our father Anba Pisentius
الاعجوبه الثانيه والخمسون لابيئنا اننا بيسنتاوس شفاعته تكون معنا ودخصنا من العدو الشرير أمين.
May his intercession be with us and save us from the crafty foe. Amen.
واتفق لى ايضا انى انيت الى البير .يوم من بعض الايام وكان وقت حن عظيم فإنا لم اخذ يومئذ معى حبل فوجدت هناك ملاك من الملائكه مضىء جدا ويده حبل فصليت انا وايالا جميعا وملا لى حرتى ماء ثم صلينا ايضا كالعاده ومسك باذن الج معى حتى حملتها على كتفى وانصرف فلما التفت بوحهى الى خلف فعرفت انه ملاك الرب على الحقيقه فسجدت الله عند ذلك واكثرت الشكر لاسمه القدوس العظيم واتيت الى مسكنى بسلام. ولمن اشبهك ايها المضوط السيد الطوباتى ابيئا العظيم انبا بيسنتاوسن لقد انك تشيه رئيس الابا يعقوب ذلك الذى قبل الله اتعايه! وذكر برب من اجل طهرة واعطاا الميراثة فى ارض الحياهة ونشبه ايضا يوسف الصديق الذى توجه الله بتاج الملك وتشبه هارون الذى توحجه الله بحلة الكبنوت وصمويلة الناظر الامين فى كبنه الرب الذى ملاه الرب من روح " النبوه منذ صلا وتشه ايضا بنى يوناداب الطوبانيين الذين حفظوا وصية ابييم
"And it happened to me also that | went to the well on a certain day. And it was the season of great heat, and | had not taken with me that day a rope. And | found a certain angel shining greatly, and in his hand a rope. Then we prayed together, and he filled my jar with water. Then we prayed again according to our customs * and he took the handle of the jar with me until | put it upon my shoulder and he went away. And when | looked behind me | perceived that it was truly an angel of the Lord, and | glorified God for it and gave great thanks to his great holy name, and | went to my abode in peace." To whom shall | compare thee, O thou blessed master our father the great Anba Pisentius? Indeed thou art like the patriarch Jacob, he of whose sufferings God took note and remembered his generosity, because of his purity, and gave him inheritance in the land of life. Thou art like also to Joseph the righteous, on whom God bestowed a royal crown Thou art like Aaron on whom God bestowed the office of the priesthood, and Samuel the faithful overseer over the priests of the Lord, he whom the Lord filled with the * spirit of prophecy from his childhood. And thou art like also to the blessed sons of Jonadab who observed the commandments


[p. 465] {#po.22.p465}
لاك حللت فردوس النعيم بعقلك المرتفع الروحانى واكلت من شجرةٍ الحياه وصرت فارس فى حكمة المسيح الله ورائض فى معرفة سرائره المقدسه طلبت اولا ملكوت الله وبر فتضاعفت لديك جزيل مواهبه وازدادت عليك عطابانن الروحانيه وابدك بالمعجزات واقواك حتى هزمت الكنعانيين وقهرت ابليس وحنوده المحاربين والمعاندين ووقفت على حقيقه أخبار الابا السالفين الصالحين وتعلمت جميع العلوم الالبيه وفهمت كل الاعمال. الانجيليه وتفاسيرها المحييه حتى تعجب من حكمتك جميع سكان الارض واشرافها حقا لقد انك البار الذى اوجد الله فى حيلنا والرقيب الذى شرف به عضرا وزماننا وصارت :رقا محبين للمساكين فى ابامك الزاهره الغامضة كمثل ابينا اننا بخوم واخرج الله برك مثل النور واحكامك قومت الظبر وحفظت وصايا الله وعملت مرضاته ووجدت عنده داله عظيمه لكى تشفع فى جميع العالم وتناشد الكل ان يرجعوا الى الرب بالتوبه النقيه والدموع المره لانك عملت المخلفين ان يرجعوا الى الله خالقهم ورددتهم عن نفاقبم واقمت الساقطين واشفيت المرضا بصلواتك الركيه واخرجت الشياطين الرديه وتاليت فى برالله بكلماتك
of their father. Thou didst enter the paradise of grace by thy lofty spiritual understanding, and didst eat of the tree of life. And thou wast of gracious speech by the wisdom of Christ the God, and a teacher in the knowledge of the holy mysteries. First thou didst seek the kingdom of God and his righteousness, and generous gifts were added to thee, and spiritual gifts bestowed upon thee, and at thy hands were wonders and mighty works so that thou didst put to flight the Canaanites and didst conquer the devil and his army of warriors and the rebellious ones. And thou didst perservere in the truth of the wisdom of the informing and leading upright fathers, and didst acquire all the divine sciences, and didst understand all the evangelical parables and explain them in a living way, until thou didst amaze with thy wisdom all the * earth and its great ones. Truly thou art the benefactor whom God formed in our generation, and the champion whom he honoured in our time and age. And in thy prosperous time there were many chief men loving the poor, as with our father Pakhom. And God sent forth blessing like the light and judgement like the noon-day, and thou didst observe the ordinances of God and do his will, and thou didst obtain from him great excellence so as to intercede for all the world, and didst exhort everyone to return to God by devout weeping and sincere repentance. Thou didst make creatures return to God their creator, and didst rescue them from impiety, and raise the fallen, and heal the sick by thy righteous prayers, and didst exorcise


[p. 466] {#po.22.p466}
العاليه الروحانيه واسبقت فى معرفه سرائر حكمته كارز البنان وعبق طيب فضائلك كعطر اشجار الفردوس وانتهى الى كل مكان بعطية_السيد سوع المسيح وافتخرت بعجائبك سائر المدن والقرى انت واضع الناموس كمثل موسى راس الانيا يامن ارتفع الى علو درحات الحكمة كسائر الرسل الابرار والانبيا الاطبار _يامن نبتت له اجنحه روحائيه كالنسور وطارها الى علو السموات بحسن احتباده فى تكميل سيريّه الصالحه وعبادته الفاخرية وقرب بالمعوته الالبيه والقطائك الروحائيه وانار الله عبنى تنك واضاء بسيرة عفلك حتى انك شاهدت نور الملاشكه الروحانيين ودخات فى ابواب البر الى فردوس النعيم واحببت الرب البك الله واحبك وعظم عند جميع الناس تذكارك ونجدك لانك بنيت بيتك على الصخ الثابته التى لا تتزعزع يسوع المسيح ربنا وغرست فيهم الامانه الارتدكسيه الثابته الى الابد وطابت الاوديه فى ايامك واطمئنت العالم كلها بتضرعك وصلواتك وافتخرت الديار المصريه وذاع خرك الصالح فى اقليم الشاميه اذ كنت اكملت العمل بالنواميس الالبيه والقوانبن الرسوليه وفزت على الحقيقه بالاعمال المرضيه والتعاليم الريانيه
evil demons, and wast exalted to divine honour by thy lofty discourses * of spiritual things, and didst excel in the knowledge of the secrets of his wisdom, and the diffusion of thy perfume was like the cedar of Lebanon, and like the perfume of the trees of paradise, and was spread abroad to every place by the gifts of the Lord Jesus Christ. Of thy miracles all towns and villages were proud. Thou wast a law-giver like Moses the chief of the prophets. O thou who wast exalted to the highest degrees of wisdom, like the righteous apostles and noble prophets: O thou who didst spread out wings to spiritual flights like the eagles, and didst ascend above the heavens by excellent strivings in the perfection of upright life and glorious service. Thou didst obtain divine assistance and spiritual graces, as God enlightened the eye of thy soul and cleansed the understanding of thy mind, and thou didst observe the light of the angelic spirits and didst enter by the doors of generosity into * the blessed paradise, and didst love the Lord thy God, and he loved thee and magnified thy reputation before all men, and glorified thee, because thou didst build thy house on the solid rock which is not moved, Jesus Christ our Lord, and didst plant there the orthodox faith for ever. In thy days the oases prospered, all the world was made tranquil by thy supplications and prayers, the Egyptian provinces boasted of thee, and thy excellent report reached the land of Syria, as thou didst act perfectly in accordance with the divine law and the apostolic canons and didst in truth achieve acceptable works and godly instruction by the merit of thy striving in the


[p. 467] {#po.22.p467}
بحسن اجتهادك فى خدمه المساكين ومعرفة المتضيفين وكثرد الرافه لجميع المترددين اليك ولبذا وجدت البركه التامه من الله تعالى واكمات السعى وحفظت الامانه وئلت أكليل البر على راسك واقتنيت لنفسك زرعا فى صبيون واهل بيت فى اورشليم! الرب يرحمنا بصلواته وشفر لنا خطابانا من الان والى الابد امين. الاعجوبه الثالثه والخمسون لابينا انا بيسنتاوس شفاعته معنا امين اسمعوا الان ابا الابا القديسين والاخوا الاحبا النجبا المسيحين كمال سيرةٍ هذا القديس المصطفى كان لما اراد الرب سبحانه ان ينقله من3 هذا العالم الزائل3 وينيحه فى مساكن الفرحين حيث لا فيه حزن ولا غم ولا تنهد مساكن رؤسا الاب مستقرة الرسل7 والقديسين والانيا ولانه؟ رسول كالرسل ونى كالانيا وكاهن الرب كمثل موسى
service of the poor and knowledge of the destitute, and thy greatness of compassion to all who had recourse to thee. And thus thou didst find perfect blessing from God * Most High, and didst finish thy course and hold fast the faith, and the crown of righteousness was placed on thy head, and thou didst sow seed for thyself in Sion and a posterity in Jerusalem. May the Lord have mercy on us by his prayers and forgive us our sins now and for ever. Amen. The fifty third Wonder of our father Anba Pisentius. May his intercession be with us. Amen Hear now, O holy fathers and beloved Christian brethren, the completion of the life of this beloved saint. When the Lord, - blessed be he, - saw fit to remove him from this transitory world and transfer him to the abode of those who rejoice, where is no grief or sorrow or mourning, the abode of the patriarchs, and saints, and prophets, because he was an apostle like the apostles, a prophet like the prophets, and a priest of the Lord like Moses the prophet and Aaron and Melchisedec the priest of the Most


[p. 468] {#po.22.p468}

High, it happened one day * my father was sitting in his cell alone at rest and there was no one at all with him except myself, he said to me: "O John, | suppose that my days are drawing near to migrate from this transitory world and to go the way of all my fathers before me, and the Lord calls for me this year." And | answered him saying: "God forbid this to be, that God bring upon the country of Egypt this grievous loss, that this mountain and all this land be made desolate. What didst thou see, O father, that thou dost give this news?" He said: "| saw this night that all the great ones of the orthodox bishops came to this monastery, and with them Peter and Paul, and when | saw them | threw myself prostrate at their feet * and Peter took me by my hand and raised me up upon my feet and said to me: Dost thou know who | am?" And | said to him: "No, my Lord." And he said to me: "| am Peter, and this my brother whom thou dost see is Paul the apostle, and these others who are present are

bishops come to warn thee to be ready for thy departure from this world and to receive thee to life with us. Now thy departure is near, so observe what | say to thee at this time, for this is the time of the latter end, thou wilt find rest for ever. So be careful now and strive to save thy soul and take with thee the provision needful for the difficult and dreadful way." And when he said this to me | said to him: "May this never be, O father," - and he said: "The decree is written and the judgement passed that | must go to all my fathers, but my * infirmity is prolonged, and do thou be my attendant until | go forth from this wretched world." And when he came to his last sickness whereof he died, he continued in bed and suffered much. And when it was the first of the month Abib of the fifth indiction, his illness grew more and his pain increased and he called me and said to me: "O John, thou knowest that | told thee not to permit anyone to take

لاا تدع احدا من الناس ان يذهب بصسدىة الى مكان بعيد خارج من القر الذى أنا امرت به يحفرود لى فى هذا الجل لتلا يذهبوا به الى مدينه اخرى اى مدينها قفط او" غيرها؟ ثم طلب" وتضرع وقال يا شهيد المسيح اغناطيوس اقف7 معى وساعدنى" يامن نال الاكاليل الغير مضمحله مع سائر الاصفيا كون7 معى حتى اعبر النهر النارل" الذى .يجرى امام0! ديان العدل!! لان0! الخوف الذى هناك عظيم1 وهائل حدا! فقلت له يا ابى ومن بغد الالام! الذى قبلتها فى" العالم الاصوات9! والصلوات والاتعاب”! والسهريات! الطوال"! وانت18 تيخاف من نهر" النار فاجاب! الطوبانى وقال ومن هو الذى! يخلص من عبورة النهى النارة؟ انا اقول لك7؟ لو نزل ملاك من السما لا بد له من عبوره ضرور من قبل مجئه الى الارض وينغى لى انا ان اعبرد فى وقت30 العشيه اليوم الذى!3 هوة الثالث عشر من شهر ابيب ثم قال لى اهتم نفسك حيدا4ة يا ابنى3 يوحنا ولاة3 تقلق ولما كان ليله الثامن من
my body away to another place or remove it from the tomb which | designated. Let them bury it in this mountain and not remove it to another town, whether the city of Qift or any other." Then he prayed and offered supplication and said: "O martyr of Christ, Ignatius, Come to me and strengthen my faith: bring the crown which does not decay, with the rest of the athletes. Be with me until | reach the river of fire which flows before * the just judge, where there is great and very dreadful fear." And | said to him: "O my father, after the penances thout hast endured in the world, and fastings, and prayers, and fatigues, and long vigils, dost thou fear the river of fire?" And the blessed one answered and said: "Who is safe from crossing the fiery river? - | say to thee that if an angel came from heaven he would not escape its crossing before coming to the earth." And he informed me: "I shall cross it on the evening of the thirteenth day of the month Abib." Then he said to me: "Be very careful of thyself, O my son John, and do not be disturbed." And when it was the eighth

night of the month Abib he called me again and said to me: "O John, hast thou anyone with thee?" And | said to him: "There is no-one with me * except the priest Moses and the priest Elisha who are come to enquire about thy health and hear news of thee." And my father called for Moses and said to him: "O Moses, may thy conduct be as is approved. Thou knowest how thou hast been brought up by me. Take care of all my books and papers and keep them carefully for thou wilt have need of them. Thou hast no escape from bearing this burden." Then he turned to the priest Elisha and said to him: "O Elisha, rule the brethren. Observe the rules which thou hast learned and keep to the observance of the bell at the appointed times. Supervise the brethren that they may make their prayers as directed in the monastic rule, in order that they may refresh their souls and strive with anxious care." And Elisha answered and said: "My life has approached * my fathers, and if thou dost go before me, O my father, it were better that | should die with thee. Now if the pillar on


[p. 472] {#po.22.p472}
ان اموت معك لان العمود النى نحن مستندين به! اذا ما هو سقط فقد قرب خراب حل الاساس فاين نجد اخر برعانا سواك يا سيدى الاب" الفاضل القديس ان انت مضيت فليس عليك خوف اذ كنت وسبقت وهييت سثفينتك لترسى فى المين البادية واستعديت بالحقيقه ولم قلق كما قال داود النبى ولكننا نحن نعدمك ونفتقر منك ونحن" ايتام من اليوم فاجاب الشيخ؟ ابى القديس انا بينتاوس8 وقال حقا" لم يبق لى فى9! العالم غير !! خمسة ايام اخر من ثلامه؟! اليوم فقال13 له وما الذى جرى لك فعرفنا! ابلا يا ابانا فقال لى4! من قبل ان اتحدث معك انا وقع5! على سيات ورأيت انسان نورانى قد17 :وقف امامى وقال لى ا18 بيسنتاوس بسنتوس استعد للسبير؟ لانه!" لم يبق لك سوى خمسه ايام فى هذا العالم وتجى انا ولما قال لى مثل هذا انصرف عنى والان فهوذا انا اذهب39 الى جميع اباى ولما قال لنا37 هذا الى القديس فكان منا30 صراخ عظيم الا ونكا شديد فيما بياث ولعلمنا كلنا إن34 تعدم
which we are established be taken away, then comes the down-fall of mount el-Asas. Where shall we find one who will be shepherd in thy place, O my lord and holy father, if thou dost depart? We had no fear when thou didst guide and direct thy ship to the tranquil port and thou hast prepared thyself and will never be disturbed, as David the prophet said. But we shall miss thee and lack thee, we are orphans from to-day." And the elder, my holy father Anba Pisentius, answered and said: "Verily, there does not remain to me more than five days in this world from the third hour to-day." And | said to him: "What is it that has happened to thee? - do thou tell us, O father." And he said to me: "Before | conversed with thee a heavy sleep * fell on me and | saw a ghostly man who stood before me and said: "Pisentius, Pisentius, prepare for thy journey, for there do not remain to thee more than five days in this world, and thou comest to us." And when he had said thus he went away from me. So now, behold, | go to all my fathers." And when my holy father had said this to us a great outcry and weeping arose amongst us, for we all knew that


[p. 473] {#po.22.p473}
اب رحوم! مشفق ومرشدة صالح4 وميزياة لكل الذين هم فى حميع الاحزان ومهد طريق التوبه7 لجميع الخطاا ومهتم لجميع8 المحتاجين لما" يحتاجونه من سترة اجمادهم وشبعهم من جوعبم وانا اعترف لكم قدام الرب ان ابى لما سمع بقدوم الفرس لم يبق له شىء فى منزله من القنيه بل صرف كل ماله حاحة المساكين0! والصدقات الذى أعطاها من يده والذى امرنا ان نفرتها على ذوى الفاقه والتى ارسلبا للقوم التقاة وكل مكان يعرف فيه المحتاحين وليس احد من النان تحصى عددهم سوى الله!1 وحده الذى يقدر ان يحسن مكافاته عن جميع احسانه فقات انا الحقير! يوحنا لعلك يا ابى لقد علمت اننا نرجع الى مسكننا فيما بعد دفعه اخرى بلبذا لم بق لنا شىء فاجاب ابى وقال لى نعم3! حقا نحن ترحم14 يا ولدى وكل ما اعطينالا للمساكين بعوضنا الله عنه اضعاف فماذا"! امسك! وماذا اترك من سيريّه6! العجيبه وفضائله الجزيله لكن نعود الى
we were to lose a merciful and compassionate father, orthodox and upright, a comforter to all who were in any kind of grief, one who guided in the way of repentance every sinner, and consoled all who were in need, when they sought him, with covering for their bodies, and gave them food when in hunger. | declare to you before God that when my father heard that the Persians were approaching there remained to him in the house * no property, but he spent all his possessions on the needs of the poor, and the alms which he gave with his own hand and those which he commanded us to distribute to the destitute and which he sent to the upright people in every place and caused to be distributed to those in need, of these no man could reckon the amount, only God can count the sum of his generosity. And I, the wretched John, said: "Perhaps, O my father, thou knowest that we shall afterwards go back to our dwellings once more, for nothing remains for us." And my father answered and said to me: "Yes indeed we shall go, O my son, and for all that we give to the poor God will restore to us double." And what shall | relate, and what leave out, further of his


[p. 474] {#po.22.p474}
نياحه! العجيب ونفانه التعيد! فلما رانا ابينالاً بأكيين لزم الصمت وقام تلك الليله كلباة غشيان ولم يكلم احدا منا بالجمله واقام" ثلاثة ايام وهو شاخص عناهة مفتوحه8 ولم يتكلم" ولم0! ياكل خزا ولا"! يشرب ما ولم9! ينقاب على جنيه غير انه مطروحا!! كالاموات فى! الخزانه الكبيرة! وفى ليله الثانى عش من ابيب نادانى قائلاة! يا يوحنا إحنته قائلا بارك14 على إبى قال لى إن رايت17 ان افارق الجسد ووفاتى تكون9! فى20 وقت العشيه عند غروب الشمس فى اليوم الثالث عشر من ابيب تحذر من ان احد يذهب بجدى الى غير المكان الذى اوصيتك به كما قد سبقت واعلمتك وهذن الثلاثه ابام التى اقمتها لم اكلمكم اناث كنت واقف قدام ” الله ورفعوا حسابى من تاسع ساعه بالامس وان اقول ان الرب0 صنع معى رحمه فاجاب !3 القس
wonderful and virtuous and generous life? But let us turn to his wonderful death and impressive decease. * When our father saw us weeping he kept silence and remained all that night unconscious and did not speak with any one of us at all: he remained three days gazing with his eyes open, he did not talk, or eat bread, or drink water, nor did he change his side, but lay prostrate in his cell like one dead. And in the twelfth night of Abib he called me saying, "John", and | answered him saying: "Bless me, O father." He said to me: "I perceive that | am leaving the body and my death will be in the night at sun-set on the thirteenth of Abib be careful that no-one takes away my body to another place than that which | designated to thee, as | told you before. These three days during which | have been lying here and have not conversed with you | have been present with God * from the ninth hour yesterday, and | am sure that the Lord will have mercy on me." The priest Elisha answered and


[p. 475] {#po.22.p475}
اليساوى! وقال اصنع محه .يا ابيناة وتناول .سير من القوت لان هوذا لك اربعة؟ ايام وانت لم تذوق شىء فاجاب وقال7 اننى لم" اذوق شىء من بعد الحساب9 اقول10 لكم اننى لاجل صيامى بعدا! حتى اقف قدام السيد؟! المسيح3! له المجد4! ولما اشرق النور بكر اليوم الثالك عشر من شه ابيب قال للفس البساوس! وشيوخ اخر انوا :معه ليخ ان يا اخوتى1!7 ل تعلموا"! سر انه لم إصتحبنى شىء!2 من مال أسقفيهة قفط ولمة التصقت بشىء منها بالجمله ولا27 ل سوى وديثار احد قد8 حصلته من عرق سواعدى من ايام رهينتى وانا حافظه30 الى هذا اليوم حتى !8 ان يكون كفنى من عرق سواعدى لكبلا ابقى معيرةل لمن ياتوالة بمدى وتقولوا على انه فعل ما لا يجب واماةة انتم يا اخون استروا جدى بهذا
said: "Do a favour, O father, and take a little nourishment: it is now four days since thou hast tasted anything." And he answered and said: "I will not taste anything henceforth: | say unto you that | shall not break my fast until | depart to the Lord Christ, to whom be glory." And when the dawn broke on the morning of the thirteenth of the month Abib he said to the priest Elisha and the other elders who were sitting with him: "O my brethren, you know all my affairs, that there does not remain to me any of the property of the bishopric of Qift, | do not retain any of it at all. | have nothing save a single dinar which | have gained by the work of my hands in the days of my monasticism and have kept to * this day in order that my coffin may be of the work of my hands that | may not be put to shame by those who come after me, that they may not say of my, He did that which is unseemly. Do you, O brethren, bury my


[p. 476] {#po.22.p476}
الدينار كفن ولا! ترموا فى شىء سوى الترارح الذى رطونى باسكيم الرهينه عليهة وفلسوتى ومنطقتى ومزدتى لا غير وكفنونىة وادفنونى؟ ونا اقول ان الحصن قدآ تفجح وامتلاء المكان من" الدباب الخاطفه لاجل تسيب السور وهوذا كل واحد0؟ يعلم مراد قلبه ويس بوجد امن ببكته" ولما قال الاب هنين ١! الاقوال13 شخص ساعه لم يكلم احدا منا! ثم قال اخيرا هوذا قدا اتيت كما امرت9! يا رب! ولما قال هذا سم ذاته بعلامة الصليب المقدس ١ ثلاثة مرات وقال8! باسم الاب والاين والروح القدس الاله الواحدة وفتح قاوا واسلم الروح بيد24 الله فى وقت ألمنا فى اليوم الثالث عش من شهر ابيب فحملنا” جسده المقدس وادخاتلا الى المذيم30
body with this dinar for a coffin, do not put on me anything save a shroud, and the cowl wherewith | was clothed for my monastic life, and put on me my cap, and girdle and tunic, no more, and shroud me and bury me. And | say that there is an enclosure which is ploughed up, the place is full of ravenous wolves because the fence is neglected, each one knows the evil desire of his own heart but there is none to rebuke him." And when the father had said thus he was still for an hour and did not speak at all. Then he spoke again: "| come, O Lord, as thou dost command me." And when he had said this he made the sign of the holy cross three times and said *: "In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, One God." And he opened his mouth and gave up his spirit into the hand of God at evening of the thirteenth day of the month Abib. And we took up his holy body and carried it to the altar in the holy church


[p. 477] {#po.22.p477}
بالسيعة! المقدسه التى يجبل3 الاساس وكفتلاة حسب ما اوصاناة واقمنا تلك الليله؟ كليا يقرأوا” وبرتلواة حوله بالتسابيح" والتماجيد كاستحقاقه وعد هذاا! قدسوا عليه!! باكر ثم اتبنا؟! به الى الجبل ودفنادة! فى المكان الذى امرنا ان تحفر فيه القبر وهو واقف ينظر! واقبرناا هناك باكر اليومة! الرابع عشر من شهن ابيب9! الرب يرحمنا بصلواتة وغفر لنا خطايانا بطلباته وخاصنا من جميع تجارب العدو الشرين بتضرعاته”! وقداساته وجل لنا معه حظا ونصيب فى فردوس النعيم من الان وابد الابدين امين18.
which is in mount el-Asas and put it in a coffin as he had commanded us, and remained there all night reading and dinging hymns with canticles and songs of praise as was befitting, and after that they consecrated over him. Then they brought him to the mountain and buried him in the place of which he had told us that we should dig his grave there that he might remain watching (over us), and we buried it there early on the fourteenth day of the month Abib. May the Lord have mercy on us by his prayers and forgive us our sins by his petitions and save us from all the attacks of the crafty foe by his intercessions and * his holiness, and may he unite us with him at length and make us share the paradise of those who have received grace, henceforth for ever and for all eternity and for ever and
الاعجوبه الرابعه والخمسون لاا بيسنتاوس.
ever. Amen. The fifty fourth Wonder of Anba Pisentius.
